- **metabarcoding-router**: Use for any metabarcoding how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/skills/_router))

- **metabolicpathwayheterogeneity**: Analyzes metabolic pathway heterogeneity within cell populations by calculating normalized enrichment scores (NES) for each pathway across different groups. Quantifies metabolic diversity and identifies pathways with variable activity patterns. Uses principal component analysis and GSEA to assess pathway heterogeneity, revealing subpopulation-specific metabolic states and transitions. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-ml/metabolicpathwayheterogeneity-pwwang-immunopipe) · ★30 · 5 copies)
- **metabolicpathwayheterogeneity-pwwang-immunopipe**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/metabolicpathwayheterogeneity-pwwang-immunopipe) · ★745)
- **metabolights-dataset-retrieval**: Reference for retrieving a named public MS dataset (PXD/IPX/MTBLS/XCMS accession) from ProteomeXchange or Metabolights to obtain native vendor files for compression or format-conversion experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolights-dataset-retrieval))
- **metabolights-dataset-retrieval**: Use when you have a MetaboLights dataset identifier (e.g., MTBLS1124) and need to download a specific mzML file (e.g., QC07.mzML) from the public repository for visualization, quality control assessment, or integration into a metabolomics workflow. The USI format mzspec:MTBLS1124:QC07. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolights-dataset-retrieval) · 2 copies)
- **metabolights-skill**: Submit compact MetaboLights requests for study discovery and study-level metabolomics metadata. Use when a user wants concise MetaboLights summaries ([openai/plugins](https://github.com/openai/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/life-science-research/skills/metabolights-skill) · ★8k · 25 copies)
- **metabolights-study-search**: Use this skill to search MetaboLights studies and resolve a compact summary for each matched accession through the official JSON API. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics-and-other-omics/metabolights-study-search) · ★46)
- **metabolism**: This workflow automates a **daily research paper ingestion and synthesis cycle**. Here's the operational flow: ([lamm-mit/scienceclaw](https://github.com/lamm-mit/scienceclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolism) · ★251)
- **metabolism-init**: This skill enables **knowledge metabolism initialization** for research topics through a structured Day 0 baseline-building process. ([lamm-mit/scienceclaw](https://github.com/lamm-mit/scienceclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolism-init) · ★251)
- **metabolism-prediction-model-evaluation**: Use when evaluating or comparing reactant-prediction performance for CYP450 enzymes (CYP1A2, 2A6, 2B6, 2C8, 2C9, 2C19, 2D6, 2E1, 3A4) on molecule-enzyme pairs with binary reactant labels, using AUROC as the metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolism-prediction-model-evaluation))
- **metabolism-prediction-result-aggregation**: Use when when you have run CypReact predictions on a molecular dataset against multiple CYP isoforms (e.g., 1A2, 2A6, 2B6) and need to consolidate the per-isoform output files into a single combined result file for cross-isoform comparison, visualization, or downstream metabolite identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolism-prediction-result-aggregation))
- **metabolite-abundance-drift-correction**: Use when you have a raw LCMS nontargeted metabolomics abundance table spanning multiple injections with embedded pooled technical replicate (PREF) or internal standard injections distributed across the run sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-drift-correction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-abundance-extraction-from-vendor-worksheets**: Use when source metabolomics data arrive as vendor worksheets (e.g. Metabolon Excel 'OrigScale' sheet or Nightingale Health .xlsx) and you need the abundance matrix, sample metadata, and metabolite metadata as separate tab-delimited datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-abundance-extraction-from-vendor-worksheets))
- **metabolite-abundance-matrix-filtering**: Use when when you have multiple batches of metabolomics data in SummarizedExperiment format with log-transformed abundance assays and need to: (1) remove poorly-quantified metabolites with &gt;50% missing values within each batch, (2) retain only metabolites present across all batches for cross-batch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-matrix-filtering))
- **metabolite-abundance-normalization**: Use when after loading a raw metabolite abundance table (rows=metabolites, columns=samples) from Metabolomics Workbench format and before mapping metabolites to pathway identifiers or computing enrichment statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-normalization))
- **metabolite-abundance-normalization-across-conditions**: Use when you have intracellular metabolomics abundance data (measured metabolite concentrations) from multiple biological replicates collected from two or more cell lines or conditions, and you need to create a normalized, cell-line-level metabolite dataset before computing Reaction Propensity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-normalization-across-conditions) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-abundance-normalization-comparison**: Use when you have normalized peak intensities using MetaboDirect's data preprocessing step and are preparing to perform PERMANOVA or NMDS ordination on a bacterium-phage or environmental metabolomics dataset (36+ samples). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-abundance-normalization-comparison) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-abundance-significance-verification**: Use when a preprocessed metabolite measurement matrix has two labeled groups (e.g. MYC-high vs AKT1-high prostate cancer) and you need per-metabolite significance calls (pval, adj_pval, dm) to confirm a published significance pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-abundance-significance-verification))
- **metabolite-abundance-standardization**: Use when you have a metabolomics featuredata matrix with known batch effects, matrix effects, or unwanted technical variation, and you have identified a set of negative control metabolites (e.g., spiked internal standards or blank-derived features) that are expected to show no biological signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-standardization))
- **metabolite-abundance-stratification**: Use when you have a peak-abundance matrix from FT-ICR MS (peaks as rows, samples as columns with raw peak intensities) and need to compute abundance-based diversity indices or functional diversity metrics that are sensitive to relative vs. absolute peak heights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-stratification) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-abundance-threshold-filtering**: Use when you have intracellular metabolomics data paired with constraint-based metabolic model predictions and need to identify metabolically controlled reactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-abundance-threshold-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-adduct-annotation**: Use when processing raw centroided LC-MS/MS mzML data through UmetaFlow and features must carry adduct/charge information before feature linking, re-quantification, or GNPS export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-adduct-annotation--6418a3343e4f9407))
- **metabolite-adduct-annotation**: Use when you have a preprocessed MSI dataset (measured m/z in the first column) and an organ/mode-matched metabolite database with theoretical adduct m/z values, and need metabolite/adduct annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-adduct-annotation--95769297c873031d))
- **metabolite-adduct-correlation-spatial-analysis**: Use when you have annotated mass-difference peaks with known adduct identities (via mass-matching to reference adduct tables) and possess MSI intensity matrices where each peak's abundance is measured across multiple tissue pixels or voxels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-adduct-correlation-spatial-analysis))
- **metabolite-adduct-form-enumeration**: Use when constructing a metabolite database for mass spectrometry imaging that must contain the adduct ions actually formed under AFADESI-MSI rather than the LC-MS adduct forms, given a metabolite list per organ and ionization mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-adduct-form-enumeration))
- **metabolite-annotation**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/metabolite-annotation) · ★745)
- **metabolite-annotation**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Metabolomics/bioSkills/metabolite-annotation) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-against-plant-ms-database**: Use when LC–MS peak/feature tables (e.g. matched Thermo and Waters feature tables) need compound annotation in a plant metabolomics context, and cross-platform annotation robustness must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-against-plant-ms-database))
- **metabolite-annotation-agreement-validation**: Use when you have M2S (or similar RT/mz/FI-based) match tables linking two LC-MS feature tables and independent metabolite annotations, and you need a ground-truth estimate of match correctness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-agreement-validation))
- **metabolite-annotation-api-querying**: Use when a downstream application (e.g. HMDB or a custom pipeline) needs to fetch compound annotations programmatically from CMM 3.0 — i.e. given a list of metabolite exact masses and adduct forms, you must query ceumass.eps.uspceu. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-api-querying))
- **metabolite-annotation-at-scale**: Use when you have extracted a large feature set of m/z values (hundreds to tens of thousands) from a Cardinal MSImagingExperiment object or similar MS dataset and need to assign putative metabolite identities using public structural databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-at-scale) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when evaluating or benchmarking LC-MS untargeted metabolomics peak-annotation methods against a curated ground-truth feature set, or when reproducing the KGMN vs MetDNA1 accuracy/coverage comparison on the 242-metabolite urine benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **metabolite-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when ranked candidate annotation lists exist for MS/MS spectra and you need to quantify annotation quality (precision/recall/F1 with correct-at-rank-1 as TP) or measure the impact of a re-ranking strategy such as taxonomically informed scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-benchmarking--ea15ecf8ea6448e3))
- **metabolite-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when candidate metabolite annotations must be filtered by predicted-vs-observed retention time agreement and you need an objective threshold chosen via ROC analysis on a labeled test set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-benchmarking--996e4a13514c3b4d))
- **metabolite-annotation-by-chromatographic-behavior**: Use when when you have an untargeted metabolomics dataset from HPLC–MS (e.g., mzML, NetCDF) with detected peaks of unknown identity, and you need to disambiguate or validate tentative compound annotations by comparing observed retention time against machine-learning predicted retention time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-by-chromatographic-behavior) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-candidate-filtering**: Use when a high-resolution MS metabolite annotation workflow yields too many exact-mass candidates (e.g. 43–13,984 per compound within 10 ppm) and measured or predictable ion-mobility CCS values are available to prune them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-candidate-filtering))
- **metabolite-annotation-class-categorization**: Use when starting from a raw GCxGC-TOFMS detected-peak list (e.g. 691 peaks) and you must document exclusion criteria, missing-value replacement rules, and the annotation-class breakdown of retained metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-class-categorization))
- **metabolite-annotation-class-filtering**: Use when assembling a curated set of known metabolites from HMDB-classified SMILES to train or evaluate a chemical language model and you need to exclude merely predicted/expected annotations and lipid-like molecules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-class-filtering))
- **metabolite-annotation-confidence-assignment**: Use when you have a feature intensity table with feature metadata (m/z, retention time) extracted from XCMS or MS-Dial, and you want to cross-reference each feature against a known-compound database to assign standardized confidence levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-confidence-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-confidence-level-assignment**: Use when an unassigned LC-HRMS signal (e.g. a recurrent m/z feature observed on multiple platforms such as Orbitrap Fusion and Q-ToF Impact II) has been characterized by HRMS/MS and needs a metabolite annotation with an explicit MSI confidence level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-confidence-level-assignment))
- **metabolite-annotation-confidence-level-matching**: Use when validating network-derived (e.g., MetDNA3 level 3) metabolite annotations by testing concordance with independent tools such as CFM-ID, MetFrag, MS-FINDER, and SIRIUS on feature-matched annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-confidence-level-matching))
- **metabolite-annotation-confidence-leveling**: Use when input is MS1-only full-scan LC-MS data (mzML/mzXML, e.g. from Metabolomics Workbench) lacking MS/MS acquisition, and the goal is metabolite annotations at MSI level 2/3 confidence with per-mode counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-confidence-leveling))
- **metabolite-annotation-corroboration**: Use when a KGMN or MetDNA2 annotation run produced putatively annotated unknown peaks (e.g. from NIST urine) that cannot be validated against spectral libraries or standards, and independent in silico MS/MS support is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-corroboration))
- **metabolite-annotation-coverage-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify how much of a known metabolite set a network-based annotation pipeline recovers (coverage, Top 1/3/10 correct rate, Top 3 FDR) by hiding a fraction of known annotations and testing their recovery from seed metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-coverage-analysis))
- **metabolite-annotation-cross-referencing**: Use when an annotated feature table (e.g. FBMN + ChemWalker + SIRIUS output for actinomycete strains such as BRA006, BRA010, BRA177) needs KEGG compound mapping, pathway assignment, and cross-strain pathway comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-cross-referencing))
- **metabolite-annotation-cross-validation**: Use when evaluating whether generated candidate structures plus predicted MS/MS spectra improve exact-structure assignment of held-out metabolites over accurate-mass prioritization alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-cross-validation))
- **metabolite-annotation-database-mapping**: Use when METASPACE-annotated spatial or single-cell metabolomics data must be linked to metabolite/lipid set terms for overrepresentation or set enrichment analysis while accounting for isomeric and isobaric candidate ambiguity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-database-mapping))
- **metabolite-annotation-ensemble-ranking**: Use when you have ESI/LC-MS test spectra requiring candidate metabolite ranking, pre-trained MLP (NEIMS) and GNN baseline models are available or can be trained, you seek quantified improvement over single-model average rank performance (baseline MLP shows ~339 average rank), and your evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-ensemble-ranking) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-fdr-filtering**: Use when MALDI-imaging datasets must be re-annotated with a specific formula list (e.g., matrix clusters) against a metabolite database and a controlled FDR cutoff is needed before image classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-fdr-filtering--57b01942d0921c42))
- **metabolite-annotation-fdr-filtering**: Use when building marker/query and universe sets for metabolite enrichment from METASPACE annotation tables, and an FDR-controlled significance cutoff (e.g. 0.1) must be applied per annotation database before ORA or MSEA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-fdr-filtering--d5830931f0b98b47))
- **metabolite-annotation-gain-quantification**: Decision support for whether a predicted-structure library actually expands annotation coverage: you hold a large set of experimental MS/MS spectra (e.g. from MetaboLights blood analyses) and want the fold-change in matched spectra versus a known-metabolite-only search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-gain-quantification))
- **metabolite-annotation-gptomics-bioskills**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/metabolite-annotation-gptomics-bioskills) · ★745)
- **metabolite-annotation-hmdb-metlin-matching**: Use when a shortlist of tumor-co-localized m/z peaks (Pearson r ≥ 0.7 against a massNet tumor-class prediction map) needs tentative molecular formula and adduct assignment via exact-mass search of HMDB and METLIN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-hmdb-metlin-matching))
- **metabolite-annotation-integration-across-databases**: Use when you have MZmine-aligned features with m/z and retention time, and you have generated spectral annotations from two or more database sources (e.g., GNPS/ISDB spectral matching and SIRIUS in silico structure elucidation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-integration-across-databases) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-level-assignment**: Use when a filtered/normalized margheRita mRList object derived from MS-Dial output needs feature-metabolite annotation, and MS/MS spectra for features are available for matching against the RPShort positive-mode spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-level-assignment))
- **metabolite-annotation-mapping**: Use when you have a MultiAssayExperiment object with metabolite measurements (assay slot) and basic rowData (e.g., metabolite names or mass-to-charge ratios), but lack standardized chemical identifiers or pathway assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-mapping))
- **metabolite-annotation-mapping**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS features (from MZmine2 output: feature table + MGF spectra) need molecular formulas, fingerprints, and chemical-taxonomy annotations attached to a chemical tree so annotated molecules can be traced across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-mapping))
- **metabolite-annotation-mlp-gnn-ensemble**: Use when you have ESI/LC-MS test spectra requiring metabolite annotation and need to compare ensemble-based neural network predictions (ESP) against a baseline MLP model to quantify performance gains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-mlp-gnn-ensemble) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-msi-confidence-levels**: Use when LC-MS untargeted metabolomics features (e.g. a cleaned mass_dataset object from tidyMass) need metabolite identification against curated in-house databases with standardized MSI confidence-level reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-msi-confidence-levels))
- **metabolite-annotation-network-architecture**: Use when when annotating large-scale untargeted metabolomics datasets where reference library coverage is incomplete and you need to infer metabolite identities for unannotated compounds by propagating annotations from seed metabolites (database matches or prior curation) across both. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-network-architecture) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-network-reconstruction**: Use when an LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics peak table with MS2 spectra contains annotated known metabolites (seeded from standard spectral libraries) that must be propagated to reaction-paired, spectrally similar unknown peaks via KGMN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-network-reconstruction))
- **metabolite-annotation-precision-recall-computation**: Decision support for choosing the most reliable FDR threshold when analyzing METASPACE-ML annotation results: per-dataset target and decoy annotation tables are available and reliability must be compared across default FDR thresholds. - **metabolite-annotation-propagation-benchmarking**: Use when you must quantify how the connectivity/edge density of a knowledge-driven metabolite network (MRN vs SMN vs FCN) changes annotation accuracy, estimated FDR, and propagation specificity, with decoy MS2 spectra as negative controls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-propagation-benchmarking))
- **metabolite-annotation-result-export**: Use when after running the annotateRC function on LC-MS All-ion fragmentation (AIF) features and obtaining a populated annotations object with ranked candidate matches, use this skill when you need to persist results to disk for archival, sharing, or downstream interpretation (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-result-export) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-scoring**: Use when you have a feature table with candidate metabolite annotations (m/z, retention time, chemical identifiers) from MS/MS spectra or external tools (SIRIUS, GNPS), sample metadata linking samples to organisms, and you need to prioritize candidates by both annotation quality AND biological. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-scoring**: Use when mzML files from spiked-standard experiments (e.g., standards in water or yeast backgrounds) have been searched against a spectral library and you must rank annotations by score and select the -performing scoring method and threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-scoring--942efc5b79a9e7de))
- **metabolite-annotation-scoring**: Use when a plant metabolomics LC-MS/MS peak needs a reproducible annotation score: e.g. a peak defined by m/z (e.g. 385.1118 in negative mode) plus RT (e.g. 1.27 min) whose MS/MS spectrum should be scored against a plant-specific metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-scoring--8626e228585deb63))
- **metabolite-annotation-source-tracking**: Use when building or auditing a plant-specific MS1 annotation database from multiple source libraries (KEGG, KNApSAcK, PlantCyc) and you need per-source retained counts after filtering out non-plant metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-source-tracking))
- **metabolite-annotation-strategy-comparison**: Use when each query molecule has multiple constituent spectra (e.g., three collision-energy levels) and you must decide whether to merge spectra into a consensus spectrum before candidate ranking or aggregate per-spectrum rankings afterward. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-strategy-comparison))
- **metabolite-annotation-table-generation**: Use when MS/MS spectra (e.g., .mgf peak lists or USI lists from a microbeMASST search) need putative metabolite-class annotations and MSI level-2 assignments compiled into a single tabular file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-table-generation))
- **metabolite-annotation-taxonomic-integration**: Use when you have paired metabolomics data (MS/MS spectra and feature quantification) linked to organismal or tissue taxonomy, and you want to reduce false positive annotations and improve annotation rank by filtering candidate metabolites to those chemically plausible within the given taxon. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-annotation-taxonomic-integration) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-annotation-validation**: Use when after running in silico annotation tools (SIRIUS, ISDB) or spectral library matching on your feature table, when you need to retain only annotations meeting a minimum confidence threshold. - **metabolite-annotation-verification**: Use when a genome-scale metabolic model (e.g. an SBML file from BioModels) must be assessed against community standards for metabolite/reaction annotation coverage and GPR completeness before publication or reuse. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-verification))
- **metabolite-annotation-with-metdna**: Use when you have deconvolved MS2 spectra from SWATH-MS data and need to assign metabolite identities via MetDNA's mzCloud-era metabolite-reaction-network propagation, or measure annotation success rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-annotation-with-metdna))
- **metabolite-attribute-coverage-quantification**: Use when you have a curated merged metabolite table (e.g. 391 urinary asthma metabolites from the MMS pipeline) and need to report how completely each non-systematic identifier column is populated before or after attribute retrieval and curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-attribute-coverage-quantification))
- **metabolite-auc-threshold-verification**: Use when GCxGC-TOFMS metabolomics data has produced an OPLS-DA multivariate pattern AUC plus per-metabolite AUCs with 95% CIs, and you must verify that the multivariate pattern's ROC AUC is not matched or exceeded by any single metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-auc-threshold-verification))
- **metabolite-background-set-construction**: Use when when preparing to run Over-representation Analysis (ORA) on metabolomics pathway data, after you have loaded both a metabolomics pathway database (e.g., KEGG, MetExplore) and an experimental detection list (metabolites measured in your study). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-background-set-construction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-background-set-reduction-simulation**: Use when evaluating the robustness of over-representation analysis results to the composition of the background/reference compound set, e.g. when the assay-specific background may be incomplete or when reporting how SEP proportions change as the background shrinks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-background-set-reduction-simulation))
- **metabolite-basis-function-alignment**: Use when you have a simulated MRS metabolite basis set and an experimentally acquired LCModel basis set and need to validate simulation fidelity per metabolite via ICCs. - **metabolite-batch-association-regression**: Use when you have a batch-corrected log2 metabolomics matrix (batch levels in column 1) and need to test whether a specific metabolite — e.g. citrate — still shows batch-associated intensity shifts after correction. - **metabolite-benchmark-peak-matching**: Use when you have LC-HRMS mzML files processed by a non-targeted peak-detection tool (XCMS, MZmine 2/3, MS-DIAL, OpenMS, etc.) and want to quantify how many of a known set of target molecules (47+ compounds with isotopologues) were correctly detected, aligned, and preserved with intact isotopologue. - **metabolite-binary-classification**: Use when you have a preprocessed metabolomics dataset with a binary outcome variable (e. - **metabolite-candidate-filtering**: Use when candidate metabolites have been matched against user-defined NMR ROIs from an HMDB-style chemical shift/SRRM database and must be filtered by cross-ROI match ratio, significance, and concentration before building a final categorized output table. - **metabolite-candidate-graph-construction**: Use when analyzing a complete LC-MS/MS run with per-spectrum IOKR candidate lists (candidates from PubChem by mass or predicted formula) plus a trained RankSVM retention-order model, and you must jointly identify the set of metabolites. - **metabolite-candidate-matching**: Use when you have: (1) a set of predicted candidate metabolites with known mass-to-charge ratios and chemical properties derived from a parent drug formula; (2) raw mass spectrometry data in mzML format from a sample suspected to contain those metabolites; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-candidate-matching))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-candidate-prioritization))
- **metabolite-candidate-ranking**: Use when you have an untargeted mass spectrometry spectrum (MS/MS data) and a set of candidate molecules from PubChem or similar databases, and you need to rank candidates by likelihood of being the true molecular annotation. - **metabolite-candidate-ranking-by-confidence**: Use when you have a set of candidate metabolites for an unknown compound detected in a liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry (LC-MS) experiment, predicted RTs from a trained DNN model, and access to calibration molecules (minimum 10) that connect your observed chromatographic method to a source. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-candidate-ranking-by-confidence) · 2 copies)

- **metabolite-candidate-ranking-kegg-database**: Use when an experimental MS/MS spectrum of an unknown metabolite must be matched to a known reference library (e.g. KEGG 662-compound MS/MS spectra) and ranked candidates by predicted structural similarity, especially when no exact spectral match exists. - **metabolite-candidate-ranking-with-tie-breaking**: Use when assigning a compound identity to each detected precursor-m/z feature by matching its experimental longitudinal fragment profiles (multi-collision-energy MS/MS) against CQMD candidates and ranking them by criterion factor. - **metabolite-candidate-reranking**: Use when a non-targeted metabolomics workflow has produced per-spectrum candidate compound lists (e.g. MS-Finder outputs) plus the measured retention time of the true metabolite, and you need to filter or rerank candidates by RT agreement. - **metabolite-candidate-score-normalization**: Use when you have a list of monoisotopic m/z candidates matched against a metabolite database (e.g., HMDB) plus an experimental setup (ionization mode, adduct list) and need to filter, score, and rank the candidate metabolites before annotation. - **metabolite-candidate-shift-alignment**: Use when a CRNN-reconstructed NMR plasma spectrum is misaligned relative to its target spectrum and all metabolite candidates need one global shift before local optimization. - **metabolite-concentration-quantification**: Use when targeted LC-MS/MS data include calibration-line samples with known spike concentrations in a 'concentration' column and you need absolute concentrations (not just ratios) for study samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-quantification))
- **metabolite-concentration-sampling**: Use when simulating metabolomic 1H NMR spectra for algorithm development or method validation, where the simulated concentrations must reflect a real biological variable (e.g. case-control status, age, BMI) and inter-metabolite correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-sampling))
- **metabolite-concentration-simulation**: Use when labeled pathway data is sparse (e.g. few known tomato pericarp pathways) and artificial Metabolite CNs are needed to augment training of a hyperlink predictor like NetAurHPD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-simulation))
- **metabolite-concentration-statistical-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolite-by-sample relative concentration matrix (e.g. 40 MDS family plasma samples x 38 metabolites from NMRQNet) and need to identify metabolites that differ significantly between patient and control groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-statistical-analysis))
- **metabolite-concentration-stoichiometric-mapping**: Use when you have intracellular metabolomics abundance data (absolute or relative concentrations) for multiple cell lines or conditions, a constraint-based stoichiometric metabolic model with reaction-metabolite associations, and you need to disentangle how differences in substrate concentration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-concentration-stoichiometric-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-concentration-table-normalization**: Use when a raw metabolite intensity/concentration table (e.g., 90 metabolites quantified by Chenomx from 1H-NMR spectra of hESC, EB, rosette, hNPC, and neuron samples) must be scaled before PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-table-normalization--da66d5c589d63a74))
- **metabolite-concentration-table-normalization**: Use when a metabolite concentration table (.csv or .xlsx) contains samples with unequal total signal, weight, or volume and the values must be made consistent before data transformation, scaling, or PCA/group-comparison analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-table-normalization--e2119c889659f900))
- **metabolite-concentration-time-course-extraction**: Use when you have a time-series of 1D NMR spectra (or a pseudo-2D experiment with hundreds to &gt;1000 increments) and need per-spectrum metabolite concentrations including for overlapping multiplets such as ADP/ATP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-concentration-time-course-extraction))
- **metabolite-concentration-to-spectrum-mapping**: Use when when you have a list of known metabolite concentrations and their corresponding J-coupling constants (spin systems) and need to generate realistic 1D 1H NMR spectra or 2D correlation spectra (COSY, HSQC, HMQC) for simulation, validation, or educational purposes, without access to actual. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-concentration-to-spectrum-mapping))
- **metabolite-connectivity-visualization**: Use when untargeted metabolomics spectra have been networked both conventionally (cosine &gt;= 0.7) and via MCN, and you need to visualize and export which molecule pairs were fractured into separate clusters but reconnected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-connectivity-visualization))
- **metabolite-correlation-distance-clustering**: Use when a metabolomics feature matrix (e.g. Metabolon OrigScale data) contains correlated metabolites and you need a reduced set of approximately independent representatives prior to downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-correlation-distance-clustering))
- **metabolite-correlation-network-analysis**: Use when you have a processed samples-by-metabolites table (e.g., from Viime's normalized/transformed/scaled CSV output) and need to identify and visualize pairs of metabolites whose abundance profiles co-vary across samples, e.g., correlated behavior of metabolites measured on different platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-correlation-network-analysis))
- **metabolite-correlation-network-construction**: Use when raw metabolite abundance profiles (e.g., tomato pericarp 2001/2003/2004 seasons with missing values) must be converted into a weighted/thresholded correlation graph to feed hyperlink prediction or network-based pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-correlation-network-construction))
- **metabolite-correlation-network-construction**: Use when after variance-stabilizing normalization and imputation of metabolomic data when you have a MultiAssayExperiment object containing normalized metabolite abundances and wish to identify groups of co-abundant metabolites that may share biochemical pathways or regulatory mechanisms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-correlation-network-construction))
- **metabolite-correlation-network-diffusion**: Use when a weighted Pearson-correlation Metabolite CN (undirected, |r|≥0.3, p≤0.01) must be converted into fixed-length node waveforms (e.g. l=10,000) that can be averaged into hyperlink/pathway signals for downstream signal classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-correlation-network-diffusion))
- **metabolite-correlation-network-inference**: Use when you have a samples-by-metabolites data matrix and want pairwise metabolite–metabolite dependency edges (including for unknowns without KEGG/PubChem annotations), accepting edges by a corrected significance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-correlation-network-inference))
- **metabolite-count-normalization-and-transformation**: Use when you have a raw metabolite count data frame (e.g., c57_nos2KO_mouse_countDF) and need to prepare it for univariate statistical testing (e.g., omu_summary with log_transform=TRUE), multivariate analysis (e.g., PCA), or visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-count-normalization-and-transformation))
- **metabolite-count-reconciliation**: Use when a BioTransformer run over a large chemical list must be validated against published metabolite totals, per-module counts, or a single-compound reference output such as the DEHP GUI example. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-count-reconciliation))
- **metabolite-count-verification**: Use when auditing a spectral/metabolite standards library inventory (e.g. Supplementary Table listing metabolites with chemical class and ionization mode) to validate reported metabolite counts per mode and class-level composition percentages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-count-verification))
- **metabolite-coverage-simulation**: Use when designing or validating a metabolomics pathway analysis experiment, especially when you have uncertainty about how many metabolites your detection platform will reliably measure relative to a pathway database. Use it if you want to understand whether your expected metabolite coverage (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-coverage-simulation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-cv-ratio-calculation**: Use when after normalizing a metabolomic feature matrix when you have both non-QC (study) samples and QC (quality-control) replicates in the same experiment. Use it to remove features that are poorly reproducible or show inconsistent signal across samples relative to instrument/technical variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-cv-ratio-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-data-filtering-by-missingness-threshold**: Use when preparing large-scale targeted metabolomics data (e.g. BioHEART-CT plasma, LC-MS/MS peaks integrated from MultiQuant) before hRUV/RUV-III normalization, which requires a complete (no-missing-value) metabolite-by-sample matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-data-filtering-by-missingness-threshold))
- **metabolite-data-log-transformation**: Use when you have raw or baseline-corrected metabolite abundance measurements from mass spectrometry and need to prepare them for batch effect correction (e.g., CordBat) or multivariate analysis (e.g., PCA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-data-log-transformation))
- **metabolite-database-annotation-hmdb**: Use when a peak table from NMR peak-picking/grouping (e.g. speaq 2.0) contains statistically significant peaks whose ppm values must be mapped to metabolite identities in a spectral database like HMDB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-annotation-hmdb))
- **metabolite-database-candidate-retrieval**: Use when an unknown analyte's precursor m/z (±10 ppm) or exact molecular formula is known and a candidate compound list with fingerprints is needed for downstream fingerprint-similarity ranking (e.g., MetFID annotation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-candidate-retrieval))
- **metabolite-database-compound-curation**: Use when you need to quantify what fraction of a compound database (HMDB, LipidMaps, YMDB, PubChem, ZINC) is metabolite-like, or to filter annotation candidates using the fingerprint-SVM metabolite-likeness scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-compound-curation))
- **metabolite-database-construction**: Use when when performing untargeted metabolomics annotation at scale and you need to estimate false discovery rates for candidate metabolite identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-database-construction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-database-coverage-querying**: Use when characterizing the search space of the CMM 3.0 metabolite annotation database — e.g. verifying that the refreshed coverage (332,665 experimental; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-coverage-querying))
- **metabolite-database-dump-parsing**: Use when metabolomics identifier mapping requires a local cache across HMDB, ChEBI, LIPID MAPS, KEGG and PubChem, and repeated KEGG/PubChem HTTP queries must be avoided to prevent timeouts from excessive calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-dump-parsing))
- **metabolite-database-embedding-lookup**: Use when when you have paired tandem MS spectra and known molecular structures (SMILES or fingerprints) and want to annotate novel spectra by retrieving similar structures from a reference database without relying on spectral database matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-database-embedding-lookup) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-database-file-formatting**: Use when after generating or filtering transformation products using generateTPs() or filter(), when you need to annotate MS/MS spectra using MetFrag and require a database of candidate structures (parent compounds and/or their TPs) in a format MetFrag can read. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-database-file-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-database-filtering**: Use when preparing MALDI-MSI metabolite identification for a derivatizing matrix (e.g. FMP-10) and you need to restrict the target database to molecules bearing the functional groups the matrix reacts with, or to the endogenous subset of HMDB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-filtering))
- **metabolite-database-format-replication**: Use when a study needs ROIAL-NMR to match metabolites from a reference source other than the default HMDB — e.g., BMRB — and a drop-in replacement metabolic database workbook must be generated in the program's expected format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-format-replication))
- **metabolite-database-identifier-normalization**: Use when you have metabolomics metadata in mwTab or tabular format with column headers and values that may contain database identifiers (e.g., HMDB IDs, PubChem CIDs, KEGG compound IDs) in heterogeneous or non-canonical formats (mixed case, optional prefixes, variable naming conventions). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-database-identifier-normalization))
- **metabolite-database-integration**: Use when you need to construct a reference metabolomics database from scratch or when existing public databases (HMDB, MassBank, METLIN) need to be merged into a single queryable resource for metabolite annotation in untargeted mass spectrometry analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-database-integration))
- **metabolite-database-interrogation**: Use when the user supplies one or more proton NMR spectral ROIs (e.g., peaks at 1.31–1.34 and 4.10–4.12 ppm) and needs to enumerate candidate metabolites whose reference chemical shifts match those regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-interrogation))
- **metabolite-database-json-construction**: Use when PyINETA needs a reference metabolite database for INADEQUATE-based compound identification, or when reconstructing how per-compound ambiguity (CA/CT) quantifies assignment uncertainty in BMRB-derived shift data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-json-construction))
- **metabolite-database-mass-matching**: Use when an XCMS/geoRge basepeak_finder result object (s2) of labeled base-peak m/z values exists and you need putative metabolite identities plus adduct assignments from a metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-mass-matching))
- **metabolite-database-matching**: Use when ion peaks from a spatial metabolomics feature matrix must be matched against a reference LC-MS/MS library (e.g. a 1269-metabolite mouse brain library) and each annotation must be scored by mass accuracy, isotope pattern, and adduct evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-matching))
- **metabolite-database-matching**: Use when after peak networks have been identified and clustered from INADEQUATE spectra (typically via the clustering and finding modules), use this skill when you need to assign chemical identities to unknown peak networks by comparing them against reference spectral signatures in a simulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-database-matching))
- **metabolite-database-structure-validation**: Use when downloading the Yingzhu96/MSIannotator metabolite database or preparing a custom Database sheet, to confirm sheet count, per-sheet metabolite counts, and organ/mode assignments before running MSIannotator.m. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-database-structure-validation))
- **metabolite-dataset-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw NMR metabolomics measurements paired with pre-analytical metadata (e.g., processing delay times, sample type designations [plasma vs. serum], cohort identifiers) and need to investigate how delays affect measured metabolic parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-dataset-preprocessing))
- **metabolite-detection-and-acquisition-performance-benchmarking**: Use when you have simulated or experimental mzML data from two or more fragmentation controllers (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-detection-and-acquisition-performance-benchmarking) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-detection-frequency-estimation**: Use when after loading spatial metabolomics data (from CSV, imzML, or merged positive/negative ionization modes) into an AnnData object, and before filtering metabolites or performing cross-modal integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-detection-frequency-estimation))
- **metabolite-detection-matrix-construction**: Use when after GNPS spectral library matching has been completed on a batch of MS2 spectra from public MassIVE datasets and you need to aggregate chemical annotations into a tabular format suitable for downstream comparative metabolomics, co-analysis, or chemical explorer visualizations across. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-detection-matrix-construction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-detection-stratification-by-matrix**: Use when untargeted metabolomics MS/MS spectra have been searched against the GNPS Drug Library and per-drug hit lists must be turned into detection frequencies stratified by matrix (e.g., plasma vs. feces) or time point. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-detection-stratification-by-matrix))
- **metabolite-differential-abundance-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolite-by-sample abundance matrix (e.g. example_data.csv with control and mutant lung cancer group labels) and need a statistically filtered significant-metabolite query set to drive Fisher's Exact Test pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-differential-abundance-analysis))
- **metabolite-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when a metabolomics abundance matrix (imputed, log2-transformed, auto-scaled) must be reduced to a list of DA metabolites via statistical testing before over-representation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-differential-abundance-testing))
- **metabolite-differential-abundance-zscore-analysis**: Use when raw untargeted metabolomics peak tables (e.g. XCMS positive/negative mode intensity matrices comparing one patient to biofluid-specific controls) must be normalized, filtered, and converted into DAM calls and gene-level metabolic enrichment (ME) inputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-differential-abundance-zscore-analysis))
- **metabolite-direction-classification**: Use when you have a metabolite abundance matrix with disease/wild-type versus control groups and need to flag each metabolite as differentially abundant (increased/decreased) using p &lt; 0.05 and |log2FC| &gt; 1 thresholds before pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-direction-classification))
- **metabolite-direction-of-change-classification**: Use when comparing metabolite profiles from GCxGC-TOFMS studies of acute enteric fever versus chronic carriage to determine which metabolites are unique to carriage versus shared with discordant/concordant direction of change. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-direction-of-change-classification))
- **metabolite-disease-correlation-computation**: Use when after training a DeepMSProfiler deep learning model and generating per-sample predictions and metabolite signal intensities, use this skill when you need to: (1) identify which metabolites are most strongly associated with each disease class, (2) generate publication-ready visualizations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-disease-correlation-computation))
- **metabolite-distance-metric-calculation**: Use when you have normalized peak intensity tables from FT-ICR MS data (or MetaboDirect pre-processed .csv output) with samples grouped by experimental treatments (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-distance-metric-calculation))
- **metabolite-distribution-summary-statistics**: Use when you need baseline (pre-QC) sample- and metabolite-level descriptive statistics for an NMR metabolomics matrix (e.g. Nightingale Health NMR data) to characterize distributions and decide filtering thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-distribution-summary-statistics))
- **metabolite-divergence-tabulation**: Use when two DESI-MSI developmental-gradient datasets have already been clustered (e.g., via DIMPLE) and the goal is to classify which clustered mass signatures keep the same enrichment pattern across varieties and which have shifted localization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-divergence-tabulation))
- **metabolite-duplicate-merging-with-provenance**: Use when a merged metabolite dataset built from InChIKey-based integration contains both protonated/deprotonated forms of the same compound, causing one chemical entity to occupy multiple rows with conflicting source identifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-duplicate-merging-with-provenance))
- **metabolite-edge-scoring-dbedges-bioedges**: Use when you have a measured m/z value from spatially-resolved metabolomics or mass spectrometry imaging and need to assign a molecular formula with high confidence. Use it specifically when you have access to a pre-constructed formula network (KnownSet database) linking 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-edge-scoring-dbedges-bioedges) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-edge-table-export**: Use when FT-ICR MS peak tables with assigned molecular formulas are available and ab initio biochemical transformation networks based on mass differences must be built and exported per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-edge-table-export))
- **metabolite-enrichment-categorization**: Use when INADEQUATE-annotated metabolites from 13C-labeled amino acid feeding need to be assigned to tissue-enrichment categories across multiple organs (e.g. liver, spleen, pancreas, muscle, lung) to reproduce PyINETA tissue-distribution output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-enrichment-categorization))
- **metabolite-exchange-flux-aggregation**: Use when a human genome-scale metabolic model (e.g. RECON3D loaded in cobrapy as SBML) must be modified using gut microbiota metabolism: first aggregate and normalize the microbe's external-reaction fluxes before setting exchange-reaction lower bounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-exchange-flux-aggregation))
- **metabolite-false-positive-filtering**: Use when you have ion-mobility mass spectrometry metabolomics data with putative metabolite identifications (e.g., from database matching) and want to reduce false positives by cross-validating compound identities against machine-learned CCS predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-false-positive-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-aggregation**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics feature table contains highly correlated or chemically related features (e.g. BCAAs, fatty acids) that should be collapsed into single nodes before DNEA network estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-feature-aggregation))
- **metabolite-feature-annotation**: Use when after MS1 feature extraction from mzXML files when you have: (1) a feature table with m/z, retention time, and intensity values; (2) DDA (Data-Dependent Acquisition) mzXML files containing MS2 fragmentation spectra; and (3) a reference spectral library in MSP format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-annotation-aggregation**: Use when after selecting statistically significant features from multi-assay LC-MS metabolomics datasets (e.g., via MB-VIP and permutation testing with p &lt; 0.01). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-annotation-aggregation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-annotation-mapping**: Use when after importing raw metabolomics data (e.g., from Metabolon, Nightingale, SomaLogic, or Olink platforms) into a Metaboprep object, but before quality control or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-annotation-mapping))
- **metabolite-feature-anova-analysis**: Use when you have normalized abundance data from LC-MS/MS for multiple samples classified into three or more discrete groups (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-anova-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-assembly**: Use when raw LC-MS metabolomics measurements (e.g. mzML from human plasma spike-in experiments) must be converted into per-analyte feature intensities for label-free quantification, and no feature table exists yet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-feature-assembly))
- **metabolite-feature-association-across-labels**: Use when after PuInc_seeker has identified putative incorporations (m/z features showing significant fold-change and p-value signals between labeled and unlabeled sample groups) and you need to assign base peaks—the most intense isotopologue signals—and validate isotope-pair mass gaps match. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-association-across-labels) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-class-annotation**: Use when a feature-importance output (e.g., a SHAP Top-20 list from a fine-tuned VAE on ccRCC serum metabolomics) consists of raw ion features that need to be assigned to metabolite classes before being reported or validated experimentally. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-feature-class-annotation))
- **metabolite-feature-clustering**: Use when you have spot-resolved metabolite peak/intensity matrices (e.g. from MALDI-MSI of 10X Visium-matched mouse brain or ICC sections) and need to cluster metabolite features or compare cluster counts/heterogeneity against transcript-only and integrated views. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-feature-clustering--45c2ad3fb8990f02))
- **metabolite-feature-clustering**: Use when an untargeted LC-MS peak list has already been matched to KEGG (e.g. with mWISE matchingStage over a set of adducts and fragments) and multiple peaks need to be grouped as candidates of a single metabolite before candidate filtering or diffusion scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-feature-clustering--97db1ac79e13fb6f))
- **metabolite-feature-clustering-by-retention-time**: Use when after XCMS feature detection, grouping, retention time correction, regrouping, and missing value filling on LC-MS or GC-MS data, when you have an aligned feature table with retention times and intensity profiles across multiple samples and need to collapse redundant features into. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-clustering-by-retention-time) · 4 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-column-mapping**: Use when you have peak-picked LC-MS metabolomics data in a tabular format (R data frame) with columns for mass-to-charge ratio, retention time, feature identifiers, adduct annotations, and sample measurements, but the column names do not follow a standard naming convention. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-column-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-correspondence-validation**: Use when after m/z grouping and pairwise alignment detection when you have a metabCombiner object containing candidate feature pair alignments and need to select a subset of mutually abundant, high-confidence anchors to anchor a nonlinear retention time mapping spline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-correspondence-validation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-distribution-comparison**: Use when when metabolomics data contains both QC control samples and biological samples that will be normalized together using methods like tGAM, rGAM, rLOESS, QC-RLSC, or QC-RSC. Apply this skill to verify that QC and biological sample distributions remain consistent; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-distribution-comparison))
- **metabolite-feature-extraction-and-quantification**: Use when after retention-time correction and data alignment have been completed on centroided LC-MS data (mzML or mzXML format). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-extraction-and-quantification) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-extraction-xcms**: Use when you have one or multiple raw mzXML/mzML files from DDA, DIA, or full-scan LCMS analyses and need to detect, align, and quantify metabolite features across samples to create a unified feature matrix before MS2 annotation or in-source fragment analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-extraction-xcms) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-filtering-by-missingness**: Use when you have a raw metabolite abundance matrix (e.g., from MSPrep or another LC-MS/MS pipeline) with many features and samples, and you observe that a substantial fraction of metabolites are missing (NA or zero-valued) across replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-filtering-by-missingness) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-flagging**: Use when after drift correction and before imputation, when you have a MetaboSet object with LC-MS peak abundances and need to remove features with insufficient detection consistency across QC samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-flagging) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-grouping-by-adduct-isotope**: Use when immediately after generating a feature table (m/z, retention time, intensity) from centroided mzML data when you need to collapse multiple feature detections of the same compound (arising from different ionization states, charge states, or isotope patterns) into unified empirical compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-grouping-by-adduct-isotope) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-grouping-by-compound**: Use when after XCMS feature detection and retention time correction, when you have a feature table (CSV or XCMS object) with m/z and retention time values aligned across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-grouping-by-compound) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-intensity-normalization**: Use when after imputation and batch-effect correction (OUKS steps 3–4) have been completed on your LC-MS feature-intensity table, and before statistical hypothesis testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-intensity-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-m-z-matching**: Use when you have (1) a benchmark dataset of known molecules with accurate m/z values, retention time boundaries, and isotopologue identifiers for all enviPat-predicted adducts; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-m-z-matching))
- **metabolite-feature-matching-across-datasets**: Use when you have two independent LC-MS untargeted metabolomic feature tables (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-matching-across-datasets) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-feature-matrix-manipulation**: Use when you have raw metabolomics peak intensity or concentration data in matrix form (samples as rows, metabolites as columns) and need to prepare it for normalization or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-matrix-manipulation))
- **metabolite-feature-normalization**: Use when you have loaded two or more nontargeted LCMS feature tables from the same analytical method that contain m/z, retention time, and intensity values, and these datasets exhibit differences in metadata scale, distribution, or format that could confound cross-dataset feature matching or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-feature-normalization) · 2 copies)
