- **metabolite-prediction-cyp450-enzyme-specificity**: Use when you have a small-molecule query (acetaminophen, drug candidate, xenobiotic, or other compound as SMILES or SDF) and need to predict which metabolites would be formed if that molecule were metabolized by a defined subset of the nine major human CYP450 isoforms (1A2, 2A6, 2B6, 2C8, 2C9. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-prediction-cyp450-enzyme-specificity))
- **metabolite-prediction-pathway-selection**: Use when when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to predict its metabolic fate across one or more biological systems. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-prediction-pathway-selection))
- **metabolite-prediction-threshold-determination**: Use when after training and cross-validating a regression model (e.g., neural network or Elastic Net) that predicts metabolite abundances from microbiome features, you have Spearman correlation coefficients (SCCs) for each metabolite between predicted and observed values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-prediction-threshold-determination))
- **metabolite-predictor-annotation**: Use when you have ranked feature importances (e.g., random forest top predictors) for metabolites and want to link them to known drug targets and evaluate enrichment of synergistic drug combinations against a random background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-predictor-annotation))
- **metabolite-predictor-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have trained per-metabolite random-forest models with ranked feature importances and want to quantify whether top predictors are enriched for curated SL gene pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-predictor-overlap-analysis))
- **metabolite-presence-absence-classification-evaluation**: Use when a trained NMRQNet CRNN model must be scored on held-out simulated nine-metabolite 1H NMR spectra for presence/absence identification accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-presence-absence-classification-evaluation))
- **metabolite-profile-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a metabolite concentrations matrix (samples x metabolites, e.g. introgression-line pericarp profiles) contains missing entries that must be filled before computing pairwise Pearson correlations for network construction. - **metabolite-protein-interaction-classification**: Use when gold-standard MPI pairs (piazza, reznik, STITCH ecoli/yeast) are available and features must be computed from constraint-based shadow prices to train and benchmark an MPI classifier. - **metabolite-protein-interaction-data-processing**: Use when a trans-omic interaction table (e.g. BRENDA allosteric metabolite-protein TSV) carries EC numbers in one column but the target pathway layer (KEGG rn map) requires reaction IDs, blocking cross-layer edge attachment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/metabolite-protein-interaction-data-processing))
- **metabolite-protein-network-construction**: Use when after generating metabolite-disease correlation data and protein association predictions from a deep learning metabolomics module (e.g., DeepMSProfiler's feature extraction step). - **metabolite-protein-network-mapping**: Use when a trained DeepMSProfiler ensemble has produced ensemble_RISE.npy saliency heatmaps and the goal is to identify the hidden metabolites and proteins connected to the high-contribution spectral peaks. - **metabolite-prototype-bridging**: Use when LC-MS feature tables from non-targeted metabolite identification must be linked back to prototype database entries, especially for TCM herbal components whose metabolic transformations (e.g. among components sharing the same parent nucleus) need bridging. - **metabolite-prototype-matching**: Use when an in vitro prototype feature list (RT + accurate m/z) must be matched against an in vivo metabolite feature list to call prototype components vs. same-mass RT-shifted isomers. - **metabolite-pubchemcid-annotation-mapping**: Use when after metabolite annotation has been completed (level-1 confidence via spectral library matching in margheRita or equivalent), and you need to perform pathway enrichment analysis on a subset of significant features (e. - **metabolite-pvalue-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when metabolite-level p-values (e.g. from two-tailed t-tests per compound) must be converted into a filtered list of differentially abundant metabolites for over-representation analysis. - **metabolite-pvalue-weighted-fisher-combination**: Use when metabolomics studies disclose only overall statistical results (P-value and fold-change, no raw data, error, or variance) and a meta-analysis estimate of compound-level significance is needed. - **metabolite-quality-control-filtering**: Use when after feature extraction (Asari) has produced a full feature table from mzML data, but before normalization and annotation. - **metabolite-quality-metric-assessment**: Use when after drift correction has been applied to a MetaboSet object, and before imputation and batch correction. - **metabolite-quantification-accuracy-assessment**: Use when you have executed mzExacto() on a preprocessed GC-MS dataset and need to verify that the returned dataframe correctly matches query chemicals to their m/z peaks, retention times, and quantitative measurements (area values). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-quantification-accuracy-assessment) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-rank-performance-evaluation**: Use when after training an ensemble model (MLP, GNN, or ESP) on spectral data, use this skill to measure performance on test spectra where ground-truth metabolite identities are known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-rank-performance-evaluation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-ranking-by-annotation-score**: Use when when you have generated a set of candidate metabolites for a given experimental MS/MS spectrum and need to determine which candidate is most likely to be the true metabolite. - **metabolite-ranking-by-network-centrality**: Use when when you have a directed metabolic network (digraph) and want to identify which metabolites are most central to observed perturbations; specifically when you have (1) a global network as an edge list, (2) seed nodes with known or hypothesized perturbation (e. - **metabolite-reference-library-filtering**: Use when you have defined one or more proton NMR spectral regions-of-interest (ROIs) with lower and upper chemical-shift bounds (in ppm) from an experimental NMR spectrum of a biological sample, and you need to identify which metabolites in a reference database (HMDB) have published 1H NMR shifts. - **metabolite-reference-srrm-validation**: Use when preparing or verifying the reference libraries (chemical shifts + SRRMs per metabolite, split by serum/plasma/urine/CSF/saliva/sweat/feces/tissue) that a ROI-based NMR identification program consumes. - **metabolite-retention-time-comparison**: Use when after executing retention-order prediction on a test set of metabolites, you have both predicted retention orderings and experimentally measured ground-truth retention orderings for the same set of compounds, and you need to quantify prediction accuracy to validate the model's performance. - **metabolite-sample-clustering-visualization**: Use when when you have a feature-by-sample metabolomic matrix (finalData) and corresponding sample group labels (finalLabel) and need to visualize how samples cluster together and separate by group using hierarchical clustering; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-sample-clustering-visualization))
- **metabolite-score-projection**: Use when you have Nightingale Health 1H-NMR metabolomics assay output (metabolite concentrations in a samples × features matrix) and you want to compute a published metabolic risk score or surrogate biomarker (mortality risk, metabolic age, cardiovascular event risk, type-2 diabetes risk, COVID-19. - **metabolite-seed-molecule-assignment**: Use when preannotated metabolite candidates (accurate m/z, RT, MS2) from a reaction-based pseudo-MS1 candidature step must be linked back to their parent pesticides via MS2 spectral similarity. - **metabolite-selection-result-verification**: Use when an iMAP SVM feature-selection run on the colorectal cancer serum dataset (113 metabolites, 234 samples in CRC/polyp/healthy groups) must be validated against the published 10-metabolite selection before downstream use. - **metabolite-set-activity-scoring**: Use when when you have log2-normalized, standardized peak intensity data (rows=peaks, columns=samples) with compound annotations (peak-to-metabolite mappings via KEGG/ChEBI IDs) and need to collapse individual peak signals into pathway-level summary scores for statistical comparison across. - **metabolite-set-activity-scoring**: Use when you have log2-transformed, standardized peak intensity matrices with metabolite annotations (peak ID → KEGG/ChEBI IDs) and need to test whether groups of peaks co-vary systematically within known pathways or spectral groupings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-set-activity-scoring))
- **metabolite-set-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolite intensity matrix (rows=metabolites or peaks, columns=samples) paired with metabolite-to-pathway or metabolite-to-feature-group annotations, and you want to score activity levels across pathways or metabolite groupings in a way that tolerates missing peaks and. - **metabolite-set-annotation-mapping**: Use when you have a metabolomics peak intensity matrix with feature IDs (m/z, retention time, or arbitrary peak identifiers) and need to assign these peaks to standardized metabolite databases or spectral groupings (KEGG compounds, ChEBI IDs, GNPS Molecular Families, or MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) before. - **metabolite-set-composition-validation**: Use when after constructing a background set for ORA in metabolomics: you have loaded an experimental detection list and a metabolomics pathway database, applied background-set construction logic, and need to confirm that the resulting background set has the correct size, composition, pathway. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-set-composition-validation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-set-decomposition-plage**: Use when you have peak intensity data from metabolomics experiments with annotated metabolites assigned to known groupings (KEGG pathways, Reactome, GNPS Molecular Families, or MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) and need to identify which metabolite sets change significantly across experimental comparisons while. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-set-decomposition-plage) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have differential metabolomics results (p-values and log2 fold changes) for a set of metabolites and want to identify which known metabolic pathways or metabolite sets show concerted enrichment in your condition of interest. - **metabolite-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when analyzing METASPACE-annotated spatial or single-cell metabolomics matrices (MS1-level annotations with isomer/isobar ambiguity) to identify enriched metabolite classes or pathways between conditions, e.g. steatotic vs untreated HepaRG cells. - **metabolite-set-enrichment-benchmarking**: Use when comparing pathway-ranking methods (ORA, mPLAGE, GSEA) for robustness to missing peak annotations, or when reproducing the synthetic decoy-feature benchmark from the PALS paper (DOI 10.3390/metabo11020103). - **metabolite-set-file-parsing-and-validation**: Use when when a user has prepared a custom collection of metabolite sets (e.g., from spectral fragmentation clustering, literature curation, or domain-specific grouping) in CSV or JSON format and wants to score their activity levels using PALS without modifying the core PALS codebase. - **metabolite-set-knowledgebase-interpretation**: Use when you have an LC-MS peak table (m/z features) and optionally MS2-annotated compounds and need to infer which metabolic pathways are significantly changed between conditions, without compound-level identification. - **metabolite-set-overlap-coefficient-computation**: Use when ORA has been run on the same metabolomics dataset against two or more pathway databases and you need a single number capturing how concordant the biological function calls are across databases. - **metabolite-set-significance-filtering**: Use when a feature table has metabolites grouped into sets beyond curated pathways (GNPS Molecular Families, MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) and the goal is to prioritise which sets change significantly between case and control groups. - **metabolite-signal-assignment-nmr**: Use when assigning metabolite signals in calibrated 1D 1H NMR spectra of serum/plasma (e.g. Bruker IVDr, 600 MHz) to the 22-metabolite panel via the 24 predefined spin-system bins from SMolESY-select. - **metabolite-signal-drift-detection**: Use when when you have multi-batch metabolomics data (SummarizedExperiment object with raw or log-transformed assays) and need to assess whether specific metabolites exhibit systematic signal drift across experimental run order or strong batch effects that would justify hierarchical normalisation. - **metabolite-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have an unknown compound's mass spectrum (m/z peaks and intensities) in . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-similarity-scoring))
- **metabolite-spatial-coexpression-clustering**: Use when a spatial metabolomics feature matrix (pixel X/Y coordinates x m/z ion intensities, e.g. AFAD-ESI 100 um data) is QC-filtered and the goal is metabolite-level spatial co-expression pattern discovery, not pixel-level region clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-spatial-coexpression-clustering))
- **metabolite-spectral-data-merging**: Use when you have two or more mass spectral libraries in different formats (NIST binary exports converted to MSP, MoNA downloads, RIKEN public databases, GNPS MGF, or batches of in-house standards in separate MSP files) and need to combine them with consistent metadata (SMILES, InChIKey, molecular. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-spectral-data-merging) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-spectral-matching**: Use when you have an experimental mass spectrum (or a set of spectra from LC-MS/MS data) and need to identify the underlying metabolite(s) by comparing against known reference spectra in GNPS or a local indexed repository. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-spectral-matching) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-spectrum-match-scoring**: Use when tandem MS spectra (MGF/mzXML/mzML/mzData) must be searched against a natural product structure database and matches filtered at a target-decoy FDR level, or when reproducing DEREPLICATOR+ compound/MSM counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-spectrum-match-scoring))
- **metabolite-stability-assessment-across-cohorts**: Use when you have uploaded a pre-analytical data table containing sample metadata, processing timestamps (pre- and post-centrifugation), and NMR metabolomic measurements for a cohort of peripheral blood samples (plasma/serum), and you need to quantify the magnitude and direction of metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-stability-assessment-across-cohorts))
- **metabolite-stability-database-retrieval**: Use when you have measured metabolites or lipids from blood samples (plasma or serum) and need to assess whether quantitative results may be compromised by pre-analytical handling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-stability-database-retrieval))
- **metabolite-stability-thresholding**: Use when a biobank sample cohort has pre- and postcentrifugation timestamps and you need to determine, per metabolic parameter and tube type, the delay at which a 20% concentration change occurs and flag minor vs major changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-stability-thresholding))
- **metabolite-stability-time-point-extraction**: Use when you have metabolite concentrations measured under controlled pre- or postcentrifugation delays across tube types (EDTA, LiHep, serum) and need per-metabolite 20%-change stability thresholds to QC a biobank cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-stability-time-point-extraction))
- **metabolite-stability-timepoint-analysis**: Use when NMR-quantified plasma/serum metabolite levels from blood samples subjected to controlled pre- and postcentrifugation delays need to be modeled over time, or when a sample cohort's processing-delay distributions must be translated into predicted metabolite alterations (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-stability-timepoint-analysis))
- **metabolite-standard-library-construction**: Use when authentic metabolite standards have been run in positive and negative ESI modes as mzML files and you need to construct an in-house RT/m/z library without collecting new MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-standard-library-construction))
- **metabolite-standard-library-quality-control**: Use when building or curating an in-house LC-MS authentic-standard library from mzML files and you must set peak-filtering criteria, manually review peak quality, and check for crossed/unresolvable adducts before library export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-standard-library-quality-control))
- **metabolite-standard-recovery-evaluation**: Use when a new annotation or feature-grouping tool must be quantitatively validated against standards of known identity and mass, and a per-compound recovery rate is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-standard-recovery-evaluation))
- **metabolite-stoichiometry-computation**: Use when when you have quantified intracellular metabolite abundances (LC-MS normalized values) for multiple cell lines or samples, a metabolic network model with reaction stoichiometry, and you need to predict how substrate availability translates into metabolic flux differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-stoichiometry-computation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-structural-annotation-integration**: Use when after statistical analysis (e.g., MB-PLS with permutation testing) has identified a subset of significant LC-MS features (p &lt; 0.05 or similar threshold) that require structural interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structural-annotation-integration) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-structural-class-pair-categorization**: Use when a SIMILE/cosine spectral-alignment results table must be labeled by metabolite structural class (flavonoid vs. indole, etc.) and uniquely-SIMILE pairs need MCS-category annotation and mass-delta interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structural-class-pair-categorization))
- **metabolite-structural-network-construction**: Use when after MamsiStructSearch has completed structural clustering of statistically significant LC-MS features (p &lt; 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structural-network-construction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-annotation**: Use when an LC/MS feature table contains enriched features (e.g. CA-dependent metabolites enriched in hacl-1(tm6725)) and you need to collapse CAMERA feature groups, predict formulas, and confirm tentative structures from MS2 scans. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-annotation))
- **metabolite-structure-annotation**: Use when you have chemical structures (as SMILES or molecular structure formats) of environmental compounds and need to predict their microbial metabolites and degradation pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-annotation))
- **metabolite-structure-annotation-integration**: Use when you have a set of candidate transformed structures generated by biotransformation rules (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-annotation-integration) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-atom-numbering**: Use when manually building a PathModel knowledge base (.lp file) that requires metabolite structures to be represented as ASP facts with atoms and bonds so reactions can be compared at the atom level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-atom-numbering))
- **metabolite-structure-database-matching**: Use when a metabolomics mass feature list must be annotated with candidate structures by enumerating adducts (default ~40) and matching neutral masses against a structure database within a 20 ppm tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-database-matching))
- **metabolite-structure-dereplication-against-hmdb**: Use when a COSMIC/SIRIUS annotation table of structure candidates must be reduced to high-confidence novel structures absent from HMDB or spectral libraries, or when auditing per-cohort annotation counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-dereplication-against-hmdb))
- **metabolite-structure-format-conversion**: Use when when importing candidate metabolite structures from public chemical databases (PubChem, ChEBI, etc.) for use in MAGMa-based annotation workflows, or when integrating external structure datasets that may use divergent molecular representation formats or contain non-standard chemical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-format-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-generation**: Use when CypBoM has flagged reactive bonds (〈X, Y〉 pairs with p ≥ τ = 1/(β+1)) for a substrate–CYP450 isoform pair and you must convert those BoM predictions into concrete metabolite structures with per-metabolite scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-generation))
- **metabolite-structure-generation**: Use when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to predict its metabolic fate in a specific biological context (mammalian phase I/II metabolism, human gut microbiota, or soil/aquatic microbial degradation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-generation))
- **metabolite-structure-matching-against-reference-sets**: Use when you must reproduce or compare precision/recall/Jaccard performance of CYP450 metabolite-prediction tools against a curated true-metabolite reference set like EBoMD2 or the BioTransformer hold-out dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-matching-against-reference-sets))
- **metabolite-structure-prediction**: Use when you have a parent compound (or set of compounds) in SMILES, MOL, or SDF format and need to predict plausible metabolite structures and pathways in a specific biological context (mammalian Phase I/II metabolism, human gut microbiota, or soil/aquatic microbial degradation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-prediction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-subnetwork-extraction**: Use when analyzing high-dimensional longitudinal metabolomics concentration tables (e.g., 1H-NMR profiles of sequential cell populations) and you need to identify which metabolites and covariance relationships define a specific biological transition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-subnetwork-extraction))
- **metabolite-superclass-stratified-sampling**: Use when benchmarking in silico spectral prediction or identification tools across metabolomics domains (exposomics, foodomics, human metabolomics) and you need unbiased, domain-representative test sets drawn from a larger spectral database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-superclass-stratified-sampling))
- **metabolite-synonym-reconciliation**: Use when two metabolite annotation engines (e.g. SpaMTP and METASPACE/pySM) annotate overlapping m/z peaks from the same spatial metabolomics dataset and synonym naming differences must be reconciled before computing per-database match percentages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-synonym-reconciliation))
- **metabolite-synonym-resolution**: Use when a metabolomics meta-analysis input table mixes identifier types (compound synonyms, InChIKeys, SMILES) so that the same compound appears under multiple labels and would otherwise inflate unique-ID counts and deflate vote counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-synonym-resolution))
- **metabolite-tandem-ms-library-curation**: Use when you have multiple tandem MS/MS libraries in different formats (msp, mgf) from different providers (NIST, RIKEN, MoNA, GNPS) with incomplete or inconsistent structural annotations (missing SMILES or molecular formula fields) and need to combine them into unified, polarity-specific msp files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-tandem-ms-library-curation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-target-definition**: Use when building a targeted LC-MS assay in TarMet and you must define target metabolite m/z windows from a molecular formula/adduct, or derive isotopologue m/z series for a stable-isotope tracer with a chosen isotope and max isotopologues N. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-target-definition))
- **metabolite-target-list-formatting**: Use when you have a raw LC–MS compound metadata file (xlsx or csv) with heterogeneous column names and column order, and you need to prepare it for targeted peak detection in TARDIS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-target-list-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-taxonomy-database-lookup**: Use when when you have MS/MS-annotated features from a natural extract (via SIRIUS, CANOPUS, or ISDB) and need to compute the Literature Component or Class Component of a priority rank—i. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-taxonomy-database-lookup) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-to-gene-mapping**: Use when you have metabolomic data (e.g., from LC-MS or GC-MS comparing patient to controls) showing differential abundant metabolites (DAMs), candidate genes from exome sequencing or variant calling, and access to a protein–protein or gene–gene interaction network (e.g., STRING). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-to-gene-mapping) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-to-pathway-mapping**: Use when you have a peak intensity matrix (samples × metabolites) with assigned metabolite annotations (peak ID → KEGG or ChEBI compound ID), and you need to link those identities to known metabolic pathways, molecular families, or mass2motifs before computing pathway activity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-to-pathway-mapping))
- **metabolite-to-som-annotation-mapping**: Use when comparing a metabolite/Byproduct-predicting tool against SoM-only predictors on a common benchmark, so predicted products must be converted to atom-level SoM labels (e.g., TP/(TP+FN+FP) Jaccard over CYP450 isoforms). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-to-som-annotation-mapping))
- **metabolite-trait-gwas-correction**: Use when running mGWAS of a pan-genome orthogene matrix against a large numeric phenotypic dataset (e.g. metabolomics traits per strain) and you must control false positives with trait-aware correction before nominating candidate genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/metabolite-trait-gwas-correction))
- **metabolite-trend-classification**: Use when when you have compiled p-values and fold-changes from multiple metabolomics studies and need to assign a single trend label (up/down/none) to each metabolite for meta-analysis or cross-study comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-trend-classification))
- **metabolite-trend-classification-updown-regulation**: Use when input data consist only of compound identifiers and trend/behaviour labels (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend) per study — i.e. studies that do not disclose p-values, fold-changes, or standard deviations — and a qualitative per-compound meta-analysis is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-trend-classification-updown-regulation))
- **metabolite-univariate-statistical-testing**: Use when a processed LC-MS feature table (samples x metabolite features with group labels) must be tested feature-by-feature for differential abundance between two classes, producing fold change, p-value, and adjusted p-value per feature for volcano plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-univariate-statistical-testing))
- **metabolite-variance-boxplot-visualization**: Use when you have a metabolomics matrix and an lmm2met fit object and need to visually validate that linear mixed-effects modelling reduced per-metabolite variance, as in Figure S-1/Fig. 3a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-variance-boxplot-visualization))
- **metabolite-vote-counting-qualitative-analysis**: Use when you have metabolomic results from multiple studies with only compound identifiers, directional trend labels (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend), and bibliographic references — and you want to quantify agreement on metabolite behavior direction across studies without requiring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-vote-counting-qualitative-analysis))
- **metabolite-wise-pareto-scaling**: Use when preprocessing urine metabolite intensity tables (e.g. synthetic_metabolomics.csv) for downstream integration, where metabolites differ in variance and must be scaled before MOFA or similarity network analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-wise-pareto-scaling))
- **metabolites-assignment-table-construction**: Use when the goal is to convert a cohort of raw 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz) into an automated, per-sample quantitative table for a predefined panel of ~22 abundant metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolites-assignment-table-construction))
- **metabolization-review**: Use this skill when a user asks whether a repeated capability, workflow, tool, habit, or agent behavior has become substrate through settlement, repetition, time, stability, and lower attention burden. ([GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent](https://github.com/GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolization-review))
- **metabolizing-knowledge**: Use when asked whether something delivered earlier still holds, when closing a milestone or running a periodic sweep over what was already marked verified, or when the set of live claims has grown too large to read and dead entries need retiring. DO NOT invoke to add, audit, or retire a decision record — route that to ledger:curating-decision-records. DO NOT invoke to propose removing code — route that to ledger:proving-code-is-dead. ([qbs784/ledger](https://github.com/qbs784/ledger/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolizing-knowledge))
- **metabologenomic-correlation-scoring**: Use when a strain collection has both genome-mined BGCs (with iPRESTO sub-cluster motifs or SCCs) and MS/MS data with Mass2Motifs, and the goal is to link molecular substructures to their biosynthetic sub-clusters via co-occurrence correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabologenomic-correlation-scoring))
- **metabologenomic-database-construction**: Use when you have genome FASTA or annotated genome files (antiSMASH .gbk, BOA .annotated.txt) and wish to discover ribosomally synthesized and post-translationally modified peptides (RiPPs) by integrating genomic and mass spectrometry data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabologenomic-database-construction) · 3 copies)
- **metabolome-genome-link-tabulation**: Use when you have downloaded the PoDP Zenodo JSON deposit and need to reproduce or audit the published dataset statistics (Box 1) — e.g. validating that 1,306 metagenomes, 1,268 genomes, and 42 MAGs are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-genome-link-tabulation))
- **metabolome-informed-phenotype-prediction**: Use when a labeled metabolomics feature table (samples × metabolite intensities with phenotype metadata, e.g. age, sex, region) is available and the goal is an automated, metabolome-informed predictive classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-informed-phenotype-prediction))
- **metabolome-module-feature-aggregation**: Use when you have MiMeNet biclustered microbe/metabolite modules and WGCNA modules for paired microbiome-metabolome data (e.g. IBD PRISM) and want to determine if module-level features match original features for predicting disease labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-module-feature-aggregation))
- **metabolome-wide-association-analysis**: Use when a SummarizedExperiment of metabolic feature intensities (e.g. plasma 1H NMR) plus clinical metadata (phenotype + confounders) must be screened feature-by-feature for phenotype associations with multiple-testing control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-wide-association-analysis))
- **metabolomic-biomarker-pathway-association**: Use when after marker identification (via fold-change, PLS-DA, t-test, or other feature selection methods) has produced a ranked list of metabolic features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-biomarker-pathway-association))
- **metabolomic-data-format-standardization**: Use when you have raw peak table data from liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry (LC-MS) or related metabolomic instruments, generated by one of 12 supported software tools (or already in NOREVA's standardized format), and need to prepare it for preprocessing method evaluation or biomarker. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-format-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-data-normalization-post-integration**: Use when immediately after integrating multiple metabolomic datasets from different analytical experiments into a unified feature-by-sample matrix. Batch effects manifest as systematic unwanted variations correlated with analytical batch rather than biological sample groups; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-normalization-post-integration))
- **metabolomic-data-preprocessing-optimization**: Use when you have multi-class or time-course metabolomic peak tables (with or without quality control samples and/or internal standards) and need to select the preprocessing workflow from hundreds of candidate combinations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-preprocessing-optimization))
- **metabolomic-data-quality-assessment**: Use when when you have raw metabolomics data with missing values in metabolite columns and need to prepare the dataset for downstream statistical analysis (linear mixed models, ANOVA, dimensionality reduction, or prediction models). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-quality-assessment))
- **metabolomic-data-structure-formatting**: Use when after peak detection in MZmine2 has produced an MGF file (containing MS1 and MS2 spectra) and a feature abundance table (CSV or BIOM), but before running q2-qemistree tree construction or any QIIME 2-based metabolomic analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-structure-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-data-subsetting-by-phenotype**: Use when you have a preprocessed MultiAssayExperiment with metabolite measurements across multiple phenotypic or disease subgroups, and you want to isolate one or more groups for comparison—for example, to compare AKT1-high versus MYC-high prostate tumor samples before applying diff_test for.
