- **metabolomics-annotation-count-verification**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics dataset of putative annotations must be classified by metabolite origin (peptides, halogen-containing, MDM, drugs, nutrients, plants, natural products) and the resulting category counts need to be checked against reported totals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-annotation-count-verification))
- **metabolomics-annotation-counting**: Use when comparing annotation coverage of competing open spectral libraries on the same MS2 feature table — e.g. asking how many extra annotations a new library adds over existing ones. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-annotation-counting))
- **metabolomics-annotation-labeling**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics putative-annotation table needs each compound assigned a biological-origin label to prioritize metabolites and gain preliminary insight into the studied biological system. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-annotation-labeling))
- **metabolomics-annotation-pipeline-comparison**: Use when comparing computational efficiency of annotation pipelines (e.g., MetDNA3 vs MetDNA2) on the same metabolomics datasets, or when validating a claimed fold speedup from published runtime tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-annotation-pipeline-comparison))
- **metabolomics-annotation-summary-statistics**: Use when you have annotation counts per MSI confidence level plus a total count of acquired/merged MS/MS spectra, and need to report annotation coverage and the unannotated residual. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-annotation-summary-statistics))
- **metabolomics-annotation-table-preparation**: Use when a KGMN/MetDNA2Vis unknown-annotation table is available and you must prepare MS/MS spectra of unknown metabolites for GNPS MASST searches to test recurrence across public metabolomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-annotation-table-preparation))
- **metabolomics-artifact-classification**: Use when untargeted metabolomics peak lists (e.g., XCMS centWave output from mzML files) need curation to remove EIC artifacts, or when quantifying what fraction of detected features are artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-artifact-classification))
- **metabolomics-baseline-comparator-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a response-factor inference method for relative-abundance metabolomics where no external published competitor exists, and negative-control baselines must substitute for published-method comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-baseline-comparator-benchmarking))
- **metabolomics-batch-correction-evaluation**: Use when when you have log2-transformed metabolomics data with known batch assignments and biological groupings, and you need to validate that a batch correction method (such as CordBat) has successfully removed batch-driven variation without collapsing true group differences in metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-batch-correction-evaluation))
- **metabolomics-batch-correction-pca-visualization**: Use when an uncorrected LC-MS metabolomics feature table with QC injections at fixed intervals (e.g., every 5–6 samples) shows high RSDs and scattered QC samples in PCA, and you need drift/batch correction plus visual validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-correction-pca-visualization))
- **metabolomics-batch-diagnostics-pca-plotting**: Use when raw targeted or untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data must be checked for analytical batch effects before choosing a correction model, e.g. samples appear grouped by run order. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-diagnostics-pca-plotting))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-adjustment**: Use when you have a log-transformed metabolomics featuredata matrix with missing values handled (via knn or replacement) and you observe systematic variation across batches, quality control samples, or sample order that is NOT of biological interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-adjustment))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-correction**: Use when a log2-transformed LC–MS metabolomics matrix (.csv, samples in rows, batch labels in column 1) shows batch-dependent clustering (e.g., PCA grouping by batch order) and you need to compare batch-correction models quantitatively. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-correction))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-correction**: Use when you have log-transformed, imputed metabolomics data from multiple experimental batches (rawImpute assay) that shows visible batch effects in PCA plots or signal drift across runs, and your study design includes intra-batch replicates (technical replicates within a run) and inter-batch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-correction))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-removal**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS peak intensity matrix (e.g., XCMS/CAMERA output) with a per-sample injection order vector, but batch labels are missing or there is only one batch, and batch/drift effects must be removed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-removal))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-comparison**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking peak-detection/quality-filtering outputs from competing LC-MS metabolomics tools on the same public dataset, and when validating that repeated executions produce identical counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-comparison--557643d66e4f584e))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing LC-MS metabolomics feature-extraction/annotation software head-to-head on the same raw or pre-processed data with pre-optimized parameters, e.g. urine RP(+) feature counts across JPA vs MZmine 2, XCMS, MS-DIAL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-comparison--d9eb957b361c81b2))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-count-reproduction**: Use when validating or reproducing quantitative claims from a metabolomics methods paper — e.g., '105 of 177 compounds yielded CSA spectra, 25,351 total spectra' — using the authors' own public dataset and toolchain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-count-reproduction))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-dataset-processing**: Use when benchmarking an untargeted LC-MS/MS processing pipeline against published feature lists from public MetaboLights datasets, to quantify what fraction of published features (per ionization mode) your workflow recovers and whether quantification agrees. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-dataset-processing))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-metric-computation**: Reference for quantifying annotation quality when a metabolomics annotator proposes KEGG compounds per LC-MS peak and a ground-truth compound set per assay exists; needed to reproduce per-assay TP/FP/FN tables, sensitivity (e.g. 0.63 vs xMSannotator 0.55), F1 (up to 0.49 for mWISE vs 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-metric-computation))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when a paper reports a quantitative mapping or annotation rate for a metabolomics identifier-resolution tool on a named published dataset and you need to independently verify that number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction--822617e4f552496c))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when the goal is to reproduce or validate a published metabolomics analysis — such as matching the SMART 2.0 narcotics or breast-cancer demo outputs, or replicating LLM peak-identification results on 12 narcotics m/z values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction--dc7c32b020a86efe))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when a task requires validating or reproducing published feature-alignment results for LC-HRMS metabolomics data, especially simulated ion-species swap tests comparing a reference feature list against a target ion list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction--009d292b17f0496e))
- **metabolomics-biomarker-hypothesis-testing**: Use when a two-group metabolomics comparison requires simultaneous multiple-hypothesis testing across all metabolite features with calibrated fdr2d cutoff rejection counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-biomarker-hypothesis-testing))
- **metabolomics-blank-feature-filtering**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics feature-intensity table contains blank samples alongside experimental samples and features must be flagged/removed based on blank-derived limits of detection before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-blank-feature-filtering))
- **metabolomics-blank-masking**: Use when an Asari-derived feature table contains features plausibly attributable to blanks/contaminants and a sample metadata CSV annotates each sample's sample_type (e.g. 'Blank' vs 'Unknown'). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-blank-masking))
- **metabolomics-ce-ms-router**: Use for any metabolomics — CE-MS how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. Searches 113 evidence-grounded metabolomics — CE-MS skills distilled from method papers and their code, then reads the one that fits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/packs/metabolomics/ce-ms/skills/_router))
- **metabolomics-chemical-mixture-generation-from-hmdb**: Use when you need to create realistic, diverse chemical populations for simulating LC-MS/MS acquisition strategies in a virtual environment. It is essential when you lack real metabolomics data but want to prototype and compare fragmentation strategies (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-chemical-mixture-generation-from-hmdb) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-chromatogram-alignment**: Use when multiple LC-HRMS measurement sequences from untargeted metabolomics experiments exhibit m/z and/or retention time shifts and need to be matched against a reference feature list to build a combined peak-area data matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-chromatogram-alignment))
- **metabolomics-chromatogram-object-manipulation**: Use when two or more separately imported/preprocessed 2D chromatogram objects (e.g., NetCDF-derived 2D-TICs from MetaboLights studies) must be merged into one R structure before multivariate analysis such as MPCA via m_prcomp. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-chromatogram-object-manipulation))
- **metabolomics-class-prediction-evaluation**: Use when you need to benchmark whether a metabolomics data-processing strategy (e.g., LMM fitting vs. original data vs. multilevel PLS-DA) improves downstream class-prediction performance on labeled samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-class-prediction-evaluation))
- **metabolomics-classification-modeling**: Use when a metabolomics sample-by-metabolite matrix with phenotype labels needs pathway-level feature selection and classification modeling at the individual sample level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-classification-modeling))
- **metabolomics-classifier-training**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics feature table with class labels needs supervised classification models whose accuracy must be measured on held-out test samples rather than on the full data set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-classifier-training))
- **metabolomics-classifier-training**: Use when you have a preprocessed metabolomics feature matrix (expression matrix with metabolite abundances as columns and samples as rows) with corresponding binary or multi-class sample labels, and you need to train and compare classifier performance to select the -performing model for disease. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-classifier-training) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-collection-router**: Use when an agent needs to find and apply a computational-metabolomics / LC-MS-MS skill from this collection, and optionally ground it against the source paper via Perspicacité before acting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/skills/_router))
- **metabolomics-combined-table-filtering**: Use when a scored metabCombiner combined table contains many conflicting or low-scoring feature pair alignments (e.g. 14024 FPAs) and the goal is a reduced final table of intersecting features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-combined-table-filtering))
- **metabolomics-compound-confidence-classification**: Use when a batch-corrected targeted metabolomics peak-integrated table must be screened per compound to decide which metabolites are reliable enough to report, flagging those with poor QC reproducibility or high blank background before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-compound-confidence-classification))
- **metabolomics-compound-direction-classification**: Use when contributing studies report only aggregate statistics (p-value, fold-change, sample size) per compound and you need a per-compound regulation direction label from the meta-analyzed log2 fold-change. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-compound-direction-classification))
- **metabolomics-concentration-data-transformation**: Use when a post-normalization metabolite concentration table (.csv) is right-skewed or spans a wide dynamic range and an element-wise Log10, Log2, square root, or cube root transform is needed before scaling and PCA-based data-treatment assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-concentration-data-transformation))
- **metabolomics-condition-wise-count-aggregation**: Use when an XCMS peak table from stable-isotope (13C) labeling experiments has been processed with PuInc_seeker and basepeak_finder and you need to count how many features show isotopic-distribution shifts per condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-condition-wise-count-aggregation))
- **metabolomics-confounder-adjustment**: Use when a raw metabolomics peak-intensity table (e.g., TEDDY Metabolomics Workbench ST001386) will feed network estimation or differential analysis, and sample covariates like age and sex must be removed from metabolite abundances first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-confounder-adjustment))
- **metabolomics-contrast-group-comparison**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS/MS feature quantification table with k-medoids cluster assignments from spectral similarity grouping and need feature-set level differential testing between two treatment groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-contrast-group-comparison))
- **metabolomics-correlation-network-construction**: Use when building metabolite-metabolite association networks from peak-intensity tables, or when explaining why marginal-correlation networks are denser than partial-correlation networks on the same data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-correlation-network-construction))
- **metabolomics-count-tabulation**: Use when two or more annotation strategies have already assigned results to the same MS/MS peak list (e.g., motif probability memberships and Mass Bank/NIST library hits) and you need comparable coverage counts, per-mode percentages, and a yield ratio. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-count-tabulation))
- **metabolomics-cross-dataset-correspondence**: Use when two separately peak-picked LC–MS metabolomics feature lists (e.g. XCMS output tables with RT, m/z, FI medians) need to be matched across batches, studies, or platforms without shared internal standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-cross-dataset-correspondence))
- **metabolomics-cross-study-meta-analysis**: Use when many GC/MS (or similar) metabolomics studies must be compared across broad biological categories — e.g., 'human digestive tract' vs 'bacteria' — and study metadata are heterogeneous, unstructured, or ontology-definable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-cross-study-meta-analysis))
- **metabolomics-curation**: Comprehensive toolkit for curating untargeted metabolomics features from LC-MS data. Use when analyzing metabolomics feature tables from FGCZ or other platforms, reducing misannotation rates through automated QC metrics (CV%, blank ratios), duplicate resolution, and semi-automated flagging. Supports Excel/CSV inputs with ~100-300 features and generates interactive HTML reports following FGCZ standards. ([cpanse/skills](https://github.com/cpanse/skills/tree/HEAD/metabolomics-data-analysis/skills/metabolomics-curation))
- **metabolomics-data-container-validation**: Use when a processed LC-MS feature table must be stored in a uniform, traceable object and you need sample/variable metadata to stay dimension-consistent with the intensity matrix after subsetting, filtering, or removing samples or features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-container-validation))
- **metabolomics-data-downstream-analysis**: Use when you have deposited spatio-molecular matrices (e.g., MORPHnMOL.csv from SpaceM analysis) and need to reproduce or extend the data transformations, feature extractions, and figure generation reported in a metabolomics manuscript. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-downstream-analysis))
- **metabolomics-data-exclusion-criteria-validation**: Use when you have received or published a claim about the number of metabolites excluded by a missingness-based quality filter (e.g., 'cutoff=0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-exclusion-criteria-validation))
- **metabolomics-data-filtering**: Use when putative microbial or taxon-linked MS/MS spectra (.mgf, e.g. from MZmine feature detection) need to be cross-validated against an independent dataset with a metadata contrast such as antibiotic-treated vs untreated samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-filtering))
- **metabolomics-data-format-handling**: Use when you have raw LC-MS data in mzML or equivalent binary format from a public repository (MetaboLights, MassIVE) or instrument vendor output, and need to ingest it into MetaboAnalystR 4.0 for unified LC-MS1 feature detection and MS/MS spectra processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-format-handling) · 4 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-format-validation**: Use when you have a tab-delimited metabolomics data file (raw measurement output from xcms, Sciex OS, or similar acquisition pipelines) and need to load it into mzQuality before building a SummarizedExperiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-format-validation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-formatting**: Use when after running feature clustering (Gravity) or drift correction (Blueshift) on LCMS data, when you have a processed feature table and need to standardize its structure, validate metadata completeness, enforce missing-value thresholds, and generate a QC report documenting pass/fail status. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-import-and-parsing**: Use when when you have metabolomics comparison results from one or more studies in tabular format (spreadsheet or text file) with columns for compound name/identifier, statistical p-value, relative fold-change (including negative values indicating down-regulation), study sample size (N), and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-import-and-parsing))
- **metabolomics-data-ingestion**: Use when two CSVs — a target/metadata file (sample names, group labels, optional covariates) and a features file — must be merged into an analysis-ready MSnSet object for metabolomics/proteomics analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-ingestion))
- **metabolomics-data-input-validation**: Use when when importing a tab-delimited or Sciex OS text export metabolomics dataset into mzQuality, before building the SummarizedExperiment object. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-input-validation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-integration**: Use when two standardized omics views — e.g. creatinine-normalized urine metabolomics intensities and top-10%-variable autosomal buccal DNA methylation probes — need to be integrated into shared latent factors without supervision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-integration))
- **metabolomics-data-integration-with-metabolic-networks**: Use when you have measured intracellular metabolite concentrations (e.g., via LC–MS/MS) across multiple cell lines or samples and want to predict which metabolic reactions are substrate-limited versus transcriptionally regulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-integration-with-metabolic-networks) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-integration-with-xcms**: Use when you have untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data preprocessed with XCMS and need to filter out low-quality peak integrations that could introduce false positives or noise into metabolite quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-integration-with-xcms) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-loading-and-annotation**: Use when a metabolomics dataset (e.g. from MetaboLights) must be read into R and annotated as the entry point of a reproducible maplet pipeline built on SummarizedExperiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-loading-and-annotation))
- **metabolomics-data-loading-and-formatting**: Use when you have raw metabolomics count data (e.g., from mass spectrometry or NMR experiments) in tabular format and associated sample metadata (e.g., treatment groups, experimental factors) that need to be imported into R for analysis with packages like Omu. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-loading-and-formatting))
- **metabolomics-data-loading-into-containers**: Use when you have raw metabolomics data (e.g., in standard bioinformatics formats supported by maplet) and need to initialize an analytical pipeline that will integrate multiple processing and analysis steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-loading-into-containers))
- **metabolomics-data-matrix-curation**: Reference for cleaning LC–MS untargeted metabolomics feature tables (retention time × m/z peak areas) when raw extracted data matrices contain biologically non-relevant or analytically fragile features and QC sample behavior must drive which rows survive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-matrix-curation))
- **metabolomics-data-matrix-handling**: Use when when you have raw metabolomics peak intensities or concentrations and accompanying sample metadata (batch, run order, sample type, factors of interest) and need to organize them into the featuredata, sampledata, and metabolitedata dataframes that NormalizeMets functions expect. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-matrix-handling))
- **metabolomics-data-matrix-manipulation**: Use when you need synthetic MNAR (left-censored) missingness in a complete targeted/un-targeted metabolomics matrix to benchmark imputation methods such as GSimp, QRILC, HM, or kNN-TN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-matrix-manipulation))
- **metabolomics-data-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw metabolomics peak-intensity data must be prepared for normalization — e.g. log-transforming featuredata with zeros converted to NA, then filtering and imputing missing values before applying NormQcmets/NormQcsamples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-matrix-preprocessing))
- **metabolomics-data-normalization**: Use when you have raw metabolomics data (samples × metabolic features matrix) collected across multiple analytical batches or instrument runs, and you observe evidence of technical heterogeneity—such as systematic shifts in metabolite intensities between batches, missing or zero values, or visual. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-normalization))
- **metabolomics-data-normalization**: Use when a raw or peak-picked feature table from Multi-ABLE LC-MS/MS acquisition contains sample-to-sample intensity bias that must be removed before differential expression analysis, sPLS, or rCCA on jointly generated metabolomic and lipidomic data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-normalization))
- **metabolomics-data-output-formatting**: Use when after completing feature annotation with the annotateRC function on LC–MS All-ion fragmentation (AIF) datasets, when you need to persist ranked metabolite candidates, matched ion spectra, and global summary tables to disk for archival, manual review, or integration into downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-output-formatting) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-preprocessing**: Use when a metabolite abundance table shows high variance between samples or non-normally distributed metabolite abundances, and you need variance stabilization and normalization before association or classification analysis in SMART 2.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-preprocessing--7ec48b58e622211a))
- **metabolomics-data-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw LC/HRMS data files in mzXML, mzML, or netCDF format and need to identify individual and aggregated aligned peaks with their retention time and m/z values before applying spectral deconvolution or chemical annotation. This is the obligatory first step when using IDSL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw LC-MS peak intensity / concentration values for hundreds of metabolites across two experimental groups (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-preprocessing--af5ac2492f6e11ae))
- **metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when when you have raw or partially processed metabolomics data (feature tables with sample metadata) and need to apply quality-control metrics, normalization, statistical inference, or advanced classification/variable selection without relying on a Galaxy instance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-processing))
- **metabolomics-data-processing-pipeline**: Use when you have a preprocessed peak-intensity/concentration matrix plus a sample information table (SampleID and group) and need an end-to-end metabolomics analysis from normalization to pathway interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-processing-pipeline))
- **metabolomics-data-quality-assessment**: Use when after consolidating aligned LC-MS peaks into a quantitative feature table (with m/z, retention time, and intensity values across all samples), and before proceeding to statistical analysis or functional interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-quality-assessment) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-quality-control**: Use when after loading raw metabolomics data (e.g., from Metabolon, Nightingale, Olink, or SomaLogic platforms) into a Metaboprep object and before statistical analysis or modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-quality-control))
- **metabolomics-data-quality-metrics**: Use when you have a Sciex Multiquant (≥v3.0.3) txt export containing QCpool sample measurements at multiple timepoints within a sequence, and you need to flag compounds with high technical variability or signal degradation before proceeding to statistical analysis or interpretation of. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-quality-metrics) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-representation**: Use when you have a raw MGF file containing fragmented LC-MS-MS metabolomics spectra and want to apply Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) to discover hidden topics (molecular families, biochemical patterns) across your sample set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-representation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-scaling**: Use when a normalized, transformed metabolomics table (e.g., post Log10/Log2/square-root/cube-root transformation) still spans a large dynamic range across metabolites and needs variance-scaled columns before statistical analysis or PCA-based data treatment evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-scaling))
- **metabolomics-data-simulation-from-real-datasets**: Use when the goal is to benchmark imputation algorithms on real metabolomics data by injecting a known missingness pattern (10% MNAR below the 10%-quantile LOD, 5% MAR) into complete data and scoring downstream metabolite-list concordance (MLCI). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-simulation-from-real-datasets))
- **metabolomics-data-standardization**: Use when you have raw 1D NMR spectral data (urine, worm, or other biological samples) that needs to be converted into peak tables for metabolite identification and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-standardization))
- **metabolomics-data-standardization**: Use when raw NMR metabolomics files acquired from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench need conversion into an analysis-ready format for statistical analysis, or when reproducing a published analysis from a repository study id. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-standardization))
- **metabolomics-data-structure-handling**: Use when when beginning an LC-MS/MS metabolomics analysis pipeline and you have preprocessed xcms result objects (XcmsExperiment or legacy xcmsSet) that need to be loaded into memory, validated for integrity, and prepared for feature grouping (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolomics-data-structure-handling))
- **metabolomics-data-structure-handling**: Use when you have metabolomics data already formatted as a SummarizedExperiment object containing multiple assays (raw counts, log-transformed, imputed, or normalized versions) and need to access specific assay layers alongside batch annotation metadata to perform comparisons (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-structure-handling))
- **metabolomics-data-subsetting**: Use when you need to assess how sample size affects the estimated correlation structure of high-dimensional metabolite abundances, or to build simulated null scenarios for validating multiple-testing corrections like Meff0 = alpha / alpha_f0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-subsetting))
- **metabolomics-data-summarization**: Use when TarMet tracer quantification has already produced per-sample isotopologue peak areas (e.g. for 13C6-glucose-traced glycolysis metabolites such as G6P, F6P, FBP, DHAP, Pyr, Lac) and you need a normalized fractional-labelling summary to verify pathway labelling patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-summarization))
- **metabolomics-data-table-export**: Use when PuInc_seeker (ULtag='CELL_Glc12', Ltag='CELL_Glc13') and basepeak_finder (UL.atomM=12.0, L.atomM=13.003355, ppm.s=6.5, Basepeak.minInt=2000) results exist and a flat CSV of labeled features is the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-table-export))
- **metabolomics-data-validation**: Use when you have raw or semi-processed m/z peak lists (positive and negative ion mode) and a sample metadata table, and you need to confirm they meet MetaboShiny's structural and semantic requirements before loading them into the normalization pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-validation))
- **metabolomics-database-capability-assessment**: Use when evaluating whether metabolomics data repositories support programmatic sample-level meta-analysis, e.g. comparing Metabolomics Workbench, ReDU and MetaboLights design choices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-database-capability-assessment))
- **metabolomics-database-matching**: Use when geoRge's PuInc_seeker and basepeak_finder have already produced labeled-feature base peaks on an XCMS feature table and the goal is to assign putative metabolite identities from a database such as ExampleDatabase.csv. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-database-matching))
- **metabolomics-database-search-and-formula-matching**: Use when after you have detected LC-MS features, grouped them into empirical compounds via isotope and adduct clustering (using khipu), and have accurate m/z and retention time values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-database-search-and-formula-matching) · 3 copies)
